Stage routine:
```
def _lbfgs_stage(a, tau_rel, stop_time, maxiter, tol):
    anchor = choose_anchor(a)
    v0 = make_v(a, anchor)
    funjac = closure(v)
    try:
       res = sopt.minimize(fun, v0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...})
       if res.x finite: a_new=from_v(res.x)
    except: a_new=a
    return a_new
```

```
def _tile_initial(parent, n, rng):
    P=len(parent); base=parent/sum; reps=ceil(n/P)+1; tiled=np.tile(base,reps*2)[:n];
    # optional phase: tiled = tiled[start:start+n]
    # normalize; add small positive noise
```

Random Fourier:
```
def _fourier_z(n,rng, endpoint=True, scale=1.0):
    i=np.arange(n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    K = max(6, min(40, n//10))
    for j in range(K):
       f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2)))
       # Ensure frequency not 0, maybe sometimes high.
       amp = float(rng.lognormal(mean=-0.5, sigma=0.5)) * scale
       ph = float(rng.uniform(0, 2π))
       z += amp * np.cos(2π*f*i/n + ph)
    # optional endpoint boost:
    if endpoint and n>1:
       x=i/(n-1)
       z += endpoint_amp*(1 - 2*x*(1-x)/0.25? )?? 
```

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, random
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
import scipy.optimize as sopt

class _Timeout(Exception): pass

def _get_parent():
   try:
      h=globals()['height_sequence_1']
      p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   except: p=None
   if p is None or p.size==0 or not np.isfinite(p).all():
       return np.ones(32)/32
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   p[p<0]=0
   return p

def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
   return a/s

def _fft_plans(n):
   K=2*n-1
   fc=next_fast_len(K)
   fg=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   return K,fc,fg,n-1,2*n-1

def _fft_convolve(a,n,fc):
   F=rfft(a,fc)
   c=irfft(F*F,fc)[:2*n-1]
   # clean
   c[c<0] = 0 # but may increase sum slightly? ok
   return c
```
Potential cleaning negative: If small negative, max unaffected; p for gradient okay. But c sum maybe slightly >? fine.

Fast score:
```
def _fast_score(a,n,fc, return_c=False):
   c=_fft_convolve(a,n,fc)
   m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
   if not finite: return inf
   score=2*n*m
   if return_c: return score,c
   return score
```

Exact:
```
def _exact_score(a):
   try:
      # limit length? if too large maybe use internal? But final should exact.
      return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
   except Exception:
      return math.inf
```
If a length > maybe 12000, exact slow; but we won't choose. Still, if exact too slow, we can return without? Rules want verify. We'll cap lengths ≤4096 or 8192. If parent length 192, fine. If we try 8192 exact 33M maybe ok. Use exact for final only if n≤8192; else fallback to smaller? But if best from n>8192 and no exact, maybe return it? Could risk. We can avoid >8192.

Initial functions:
```
def _a_to_z(a):
   a=np.clip(a,1e-300,None); a=a/a.sum()
   z=np.log(a)
   z = z - z.mean()
   z=np.clip(z,-8,8)
   return z
```
But L-BFGS anchor uses v not z mean. For functions only.

`_binomial_a(n)`:
```
c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1; for i in range(1,n): c[i]=c[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
return normalize(c)
```
For large n, recurrence stable. Also reverse.

`_tile_parent(parent,n,offset=0,rng=None,reverse=False)`:
```
p=normalize(parent); P=len(p)
if reverse: p=p[::-1]
if n<=P:
   # interpolate
   if n==1: return np.array([1.])
   xs=np.linspace(0,P-1,n); a=np.interp(xs,np.arange(P),p); a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
else:
   reps=n//P + 3
   tiled=np.tile(p,reps)
   if offset: tiled = tiled[:offset+n]
   a=tiled[:n]
return normalize(a)
```
If offset+n > len tiled, extend? reps enough. For n < P interpolation. Add small noise optional.

`_random_fourier_a(n,rng,variant,parent=None)`:
Use z and softmax.
```
def _softmax(z): z=z-z.max; e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s>0 return e/s; return uniform
```
Variants:
0: uniform with endpoint boost? `z = endpoint_amp*(1-4x(1-x))` plus noise.
1: random Fourier: K = max(8, min(64, n//8)); frequencies maybe sorted? amplitudes lognormal; add endpoint boost; scale by std target 0.5-1.5.
2: lowpass random walk: generate white noise, apply simple moving average with window w=n//10, scale.
3: binomial modulated by random sine envelope: start binomial a, z=log(a)+random Fourier small.
4: parent-like (if parent): z=log(tile_parent)+ random Fourier.
Ensure positive.

We can just create a function returning a for each start, then convert to v and optimize.

L-BFGS stage:
```
def _stage_lbfgs(a, tau_rel, stop_time, maxiter, plans):
   n=len(a); K,fc,fg,startg,stopeg=plans
   a=normalize(a)
   anchor = int(np.argmin(np.abs(np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - np.median(np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))))))
   mask = np.arange(n) != anchor
   v0 = np.empty(n-1)
   logs=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   zfull=np.empty(n); zfull[anchor]=0; zfull[mask]=logs[mask]-logs[anchor]
   v0=zfull[mask]; v0=np.clip(v0,-50,50)
   last_v = [v0.copy()]
   def callback(xk):
      last_v[0]=xk.copy()
      if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
          raise _Timeout
   def fun(x):
      x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
         return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
      zfull=np.empty(n)
      zfull[anchor]=0.0
      zfull[mask]=x
      # stable softmax
      zm=max(zfull)
      e=np.exp(zfull-zm)
      s=e.sum()
      if not finite or s<=0:
         a_cur=np.full(n,1/n); grad_zero... return large
      a_cur=e/s
      c=_fft_convolve(a_cur,n,fc)
      m=float(c.max())
      if not finite or m<=0: return large
      tau=tau_rel*m
      xexp=(c-m)/tau
      # xexp <=0 maybe, but numerical >0 small if c>m? no. Clip positive? `np.exp(np.minimum(xexp,0.0))`? If some c slightly above m due? m max so <=0.
      ex=np.exp(np.clip(xexp, -745, 0.0))
      ss=ex.sum()
      if ss<=0: return large
      p=ex/ss
      U=m+tau*math.log(ss)
      # gradient
      g = 2.0 * _fft_cross_grad(p, a_cur, n, fg, startg, stopeg)
      S = 2.0 * float(np.dot(p, c))
      h = a_cur * (g - S)
      if not finite: h=...; return large
      score=2.0*n*U
      # gradient wrt v
      gv = h[mask]
      # optionally scale? L-BFGS expects grad.
      if not finite: return large
      return score, gv
   try:
       res=sopt.minimize(fun, v0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=callback,
                         options={...})
       v_final=res.x if finite else last_v[0]
   except _Timeout:
       v_final=last_v[0]
   except Exception:
       v_final=v0
   # convert
   zfull=np.empty(n); zfull[anchor]=0; zfull[mask]=v_final
   e=exp(zfull-max); a_new=e/sum
   return a_new
```
Need implement `_fft_cross_grad(p,a,n,fg,startg,stopeg)`:
```
a_rev = a[::-1]
Fp = rfft(p, fg)
Fa = rfft(a_rev, fg)
h = irfft(Fp*Fa, fg)[startg:stopeg]  # length n
return h
```
Check `startg=n-1`, `stopeg=2*n-1`. Yes. If fg too small? next_fast_len(3n-2) exactly. Good.

Potential performance: In each objective, `rfft(p,fg)` and `rfft(a_rev,fg)`. Could precompute FFT of p? p changes each call. okay.

```
      c_top=c[idx]; mtop=max
      p = softmax((c_top - mtmax)/max(1e-12,mmax*0.005))
      # loop gradient over idx
      hg=zeros
      for k,w in zip(idx,p):
         if w<1e-12: continue
         j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(n-1,k)

      maxh=np.max(np.abs(h)); if maxh<1e-8: break
      d=h/maxh
      # try eta values
```

Implement `_rand_fourier_a(n,rng, parent=None, tail=False)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)
z=np.zeros(n)
# number harmonics
K=max(5, min(60, n//6))
for _ in range(K):
   f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2)))
   amp = float(rng.lognormal(-0.6,0.6))
   ph = float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   z += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/n + ph)
# endpoint boost
if n>1:
   t=x/(n-1)
   A=float(rng.uniform(0.0,0.7))
   z += A*(1 - 4*t*(1-t))
# small noise
z += rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
# scale to std target
std = float(z.std())
target = float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.5))
if std>1e-8: z *= target/std
z -= z.mean(); clip(-6,6)
a=_softmax_z(z)
# maybe multiply parent log if provided:
if parent is not None:
   base = _tile_parent(parent,n)
   mix = float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))
   a = (mix*base + (1-mix)*a)/...
return a
```

Detailed functions:

```
def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    a[np.isnan(a) | np.isinf(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size)
    return a / s
```
If a.size=0? not.

`_softmax_from_z`:
```
def _softmax_from_z(z, clip=60.0):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=z.size
   if n==0: return np.array([1.0])
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
   z=np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
   z = z - z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   return e/s
```
If after clip all -60, max=0? fine.

`_z_from_a`:
```
def _z_from_a(a, mean_zero=True, clip=60):
   a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float).ravel(),1e-300,None)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones_like(a)
   a=a/s
   z=np.log(a)
   if mean_zero: z-=z.mean()
   return np.clip(z,-clip,clip)
```

`_fft_plans(n)`: as above.

`_fft_convolve`:
```
def _fft_convolve(a, n, fc):
    F = rfft(a, fc)
    c = irfft(F * F, fc)
    c = c[:2*n-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```

`_fft_cross_grad`:
```
def _fft_cross_grad(p, a, n, fg, start, stop):
    ar = a[::-1]
    Fp = rfft(p, fg)
    Fa = rfft(ar, fg)
    h = irfft(Fp * Fa, fg)[start:stop]
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
    h[h < 0.0] = 0.0
    return h
```

`_fast_score(a,n,fc, return_c=False)`:
```
c=_fft_convolve(a,n,fc); m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0; if not finite: score=inf else 2.0*n*m
```

`_binomial_a`:
```
def _binomial_a(n):
   c=np.empty(n)
   c[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n):
      c[i]=c[i-1]*(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
   return _normalize(c)
```
For n large, loop 4096 okay. Could vectorize? no need.

`_tile_parent`:
```
def _tile_parent(p, n, reverse=False, offset=0, rng=None, noise=0.0):
   p=_normalize(np.asarray(p,float).ravel())
   if p.size <1: return _binomial_a(n)
   P=p.size
   if reverse: p=p[::-1]
   if n <= P:
       if n == 1: return np.array([1.0])
       xs=np.linspace(0.0,P-1.0,n)
       a=np.interp(xs, np.arange(P), p)
   else:
       reps=(n + 2*P)//P + 3
       long=np.tile(p, reps)
       off=int(offset)%max(1,P)
       if off+n > long.size:
           long=np.concatenate([long, np.tile(p, (off+n-long.size)//P + 2)])
       a=long[off:off+n]
       if a.size < n:
           a=np.pad(a, (0,n-a.size), 'edge')
   if noise>0 and rng is not None:
       a = a + noise * max(1.0, a.mean()) * rng.standard_normal(n)
   return _normalize(np.maximum(a,0.0))
```
For n<=P and reverse, interpolation reversed order; okay.

Random Fourier:

```
def _random_fourier_a(n, rng, use_parent=None, mix=None):
   i=np.arange(n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   K=max(5, min(60, n//8))
   for _ in range(K):
       f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2)))
       amp=float(rng.lognormal(mean=-0.6, sigma=0.5))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0, 2*math.pi))
       z += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*f*i/n + ph)
   if n>1:
      t=i/(n-1)
      A=float(rng.uniform(0.0,0.8))
      z += A*(1 - 4*t*(1-t))
   z += rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)
   std=float(np.std(z))
   target=float(rng.uniform(0.4,1.4))
   if std>1e-12: z *= target/std
   z -= z.mean()
   a=_softmax_from_z(z)
   if use_parent is not None:
      base=_phase_best_parent(use_parent,n,fc?) # too expensive maybe use simple tile offset0
      base=_tile_parent(use_parent,n)
      mixv=float(rng.uniform(0.4,1.0)) if mix is None else mix
      a = (mixv*base + (1-mixv)*a)
      a=_normalize(a)
   return a
```
But calling `_phase_best_parent` in random mix too expensive; use simple `_tile_parent` offset rng.integers(0,L). Good.

```
def _optimize_start(a0, n, stop_time, plans, consider):
    a0=normalize; consider(a0)
    K,fc,fg,startg,stopeg=plans
    a_cur=a0; a_best=a0.copy(); best_fast=fast(a0)
    tau_list = (0.35,0.18,0.09,0.045,0.022,0.011,0.0055,0.0027)
    if n>1024: tau_list=tau_list[:5]
    maxiter = 160 if n<=512 else 100
    for i,tau in enumerate(tau_list):
       if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1: break
       rem = stop_time - time.monotonic()
       stage_stop = min(stop_time - 0.2, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, rem/ max(1,len(tau_list)-i)))
       a_new = _lbfgs_stage(a_cur, tau, stage_stop, maxiter, plans)
       if a_new is None: break
       s_new = fast(a_new)
       if s_new < best_fast:
          best_fast=a_new.copy(); # careful a_new is array, copy
          a_best=a_new.copy()
       if s_new < best_fast * 1.001:
          a_cur=a_new
       else:
          # keep best? maybe use a_new? choose a_best
          a_cur=a_best
       consider(a_new if s_new<best_fast else a_best) # maybe consider a_cur
       # consider sym
       sym=0.5*(a_cur+a_cur[::-1]); consider(sym)
    return a_best,best_fast
```

Now, implement `_random_start` without fc:
```
def _random_start(n, idx, base_parent):
    var = idx
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123456 + n*1000003 + idx*104729)
    if var % 7 == 0:
       return _random_fourier_a(n,rng,None)
    elif var % 7 == 1:
       return _random_fourier_a(n,rng,base_parent)
    elif var % 7 == 2:
       return _binomial_perturbed(n,rng)
    elif var % 7 == 3:
       # lowpass random walk
       w=max(3, n//20)
       z=np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,1,n)); z=np.convolve(z,np.ones(w)/w,'same')
       # endpoint boost etc; softmax
    elif var % 7 == 4:
       # tile parent with random tile scaling? maybe
       base=_tile_parent(base_parent,n,offset=rng.integers(0,base_parent.size) if base_parent.size>0 else 0)
       z=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300)) + _random_fourier_z_small(n,rng)
       return _softmax_from_z(z)
    else: uniform with endpoint boost.
```
Keep not too complex.

Need `_random_fourier_z(n,rng, scale)` helper for small.

Let's create `_rand_fourier_z(n, rng, K=None, endpoint=True, scale=1.0)`:
```
def _rand_fourier_z(n,rng,scale=1.0,endpoint=True):
   i=np.arange(n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   K=max(5, min(50, n//8))
   for _ in range(K): ...
   if endpoint: z += rng.uniform(0,0.6)*(1-4*t*(1-t))
   z += rng.normal(0,0.03,n)
   std=...; target=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)*scale
   if std>0: z*=target/std
   z-=z.mean()
   return np.clip(z,-8,8)
```
Then `_random_fourier_a` uses `a=softmax(z)` and mix.

`_binomial_perturbed`:
```
b=_binomial_a(n)
z=np.log(np.maximum(b,1e-300))
z += 0.3*_rand_fourier_z(n,rng,scale=0.5,endpoint=False)
z-=z.mean(); return softmax(z)
```

Uniform endpoint start:
```
t=i/(n-1); z=A*(1-4*t*(1-t)) + noise; softmax
```

```
def _local_search(a, stop_time, n, fc):
    a=normalize; z=np.log(a)-mean; best=a.copy(); best_s=fast; rng=default_rng(999+n)
    step_sizes=[0.8,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
    Kmax = max(16, min(64, n//3))
    for it in range(500):
       if time>stop_time-1: break
       c=fft_conv; M=m
       # top K
       if Kmax >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
       else: idx=np.argpartition(-c, Kmax-1)[:Kmax]
       # weights: softmax of (c_top-M)/tau_active
       ct=c[idx]; mct=ct.max(); if mct<=0: break; tau=max(1e-8, 0.01*mct)
       ex=np.exp((ct-mct)/tau); p=ex/ex.sum()
       hg=np.zeros(n)
       for k,w in zip(idx,p):
           if w <= 1e-10: continue
           j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(n-1,k)
           if j1<j0: continue
           j=np.arange(j0,j1+1)
           hg[j0:j1+1] += 2.0*w*a[k-j]
       if not finite: break
       S=float(np.dot(a,hg))
       h=a*(hg-S)
       mh=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if mh<1e-10: break
       d=h/mh
       accepted=False
       for st in step_sizes:
           zc=z-st*d
           ac=softmax(zc)
           sc=fast(ac,n,fc)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
              z=zc; a=ac.copy(); best_a=ac.copy(); best_s=sc; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # perturb
           z += rng.normal(0, 0.02/np.sqrt(n), n)
           z-=z.mean()
    return best_a,best_s
```
Step sizes: direction normalized max 1, z step 0.8 may be large; okay. Could reduce `bh` not sum zero due h sum zero, z mean remains? d sum maybe zero? h sum ~0, d sum ~0. Good.

```
def _try_shifts(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); if a.size<2: return
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; fc=None? compute
   # check time
   b0=a; s0=fast
   # sym
   sym=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); s=fast(sym); update
   step=max(1, n//256)
   # evaluate up to max 512 shifts
   cnt=0
   for off in range(0,n,step):
       if cnt>800 or time>hard? break
       b=np.roll(b0, off)  # use best local (maybe sym)
       s=fast(b)
       if s<best_s: best=b
       cnt++
   _consider(best, fast=best_s, fc=fc)
```

Let's code `_rand_fourier_z`:
```
def _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, scale=1.0, endpoint=True):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = max(5, min(50, n // 8))
    for _ in range(K):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2)))
        amp = float(rng.lognormal(mean=-0.6, sigma=0.5))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * f * i) / n + ph)
    if endpoint and n > 1:
        t = i / (n - 1.0)
        z += float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.7)) * (1.0 - 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t))
    z += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    std = float(np.std(z))
    target = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5)) * scale
    if std > 1e-12:
        z *= target / std
    z -= z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)
```
If n=1, K? max(5,min(50,0)) =5, f random 1..1, cos 2π*f*i/n=cos0, okay; endpoint no. But n≥64.

`_random_start`:
```
def _random_start(n, var, parent):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321 + n*1000003 + var*104729)
    v = int(var) % 7
    if v == 0:
       z = _rand_fourier_z(n,rng, endpoint=True)
       return _softmax_from_z(z)
    elif v == 1:
       z = _rand_fourier_z(n,rng, endpoint=True, scale=1.2)
       a = _softmax_from_z(z)
       if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
          off = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,parent.size)))
          base = _tile_parent(parent,n,offset=off)
          mix = float(rng.uniform(0.35,0.85))
          a = mix*base + (1-mix)*a
          a = _normalize(a)
       return a
    elif v == 2:
       b = _binomial_a(n)
       z = np.log(np.maximum(b,1e-300)) + 0.4*_rand_fourier_z(n,rng, endpoint=False, scale=0.6)
       z -= z.mean(); return _softmax_from_z(z)
    elif v == 3:
       # lowpass random
       w = max(3, n//20)
       z = np.cumsum(rng.standard_normal(n))
       # simple moving average
       if w < n:
           z = np.convolve(z, np.ones(w)/w, mode='same')
       z = z - z.mean()
       std = float(np.std(z))
       if std > 1e-12: z *= (float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.2))/std)
       t = np.arange(n)/(max(1,n-1))
       z += float(rng.uniform(0,0.5))*(1-4*t*(1-t))
       return _softmax_from_z(np.clip(z,-8,8))
    elif v == 4:
       # parent tile log + endpoint boost
       if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
          off=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,parent.size)))
          base=_tile_parent(parent,n,offset=off)
       else: base=_binomial_a(n)
       z=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300))
       z += 0.5*_rand_fourier_z(n,rng, endpoint=True, scale=0.5)
       z -= z.mean(); return _softmax_from_z(z)
    elif v == 5:
       # two sinusoidal modulated binomial
       b=_binomial_a(n); z=np.log(...); i=...; K=3 add sines; softmax
    else:
       t=np.arange(n)/(max(1,n-1)); A=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)); z=A*(1-4*t*(1-t))+rng.normal(0,0.08,n); return softmax
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
import scipy.optimize as sopt


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
    if p is None or p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(p).all():
        p = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    return p


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _softmax_from_z(z, clip=60.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _z_from_a(a, mean_zero=True, clip=60.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-300, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s
    z = np.log(a)
    if mean_zero:
        z -= float(z.mean())
    return np.clip(z, -clip, clip)


def _fft_plans(n):
    n = int(n)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    fc = next_fast_len(max(1, K))
    fg = next_fast_len(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    return K, fc, fg, n - 1, 2 * n - 1


def _fft_convolve(a, n, fc):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    F = rfft(a[:n], fc)
    c = irfft(F * F, fc)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _fft_cross_grad(p, a, n, fg, start, stop):
    ar = a[::-1]
    Fp = rfft(p, fg)
    Fa = rfft(ar, fg)
    h = irfft(Fp * Fa, fg)[start:stop]
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
    h[h < 0.0] = 0.0
    return h


def _fast_score(a, n, fc, return_c=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = _fft_convolve(a, n, fc)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        if return_c:
            return math.inf, c
        return math.inf
    score = float(2.0 * n * m)
    if return_c:
        return score, c
    return score


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if ev is None:
            return math.inf
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        return float(ev([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _binomial_a(n):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return _normalize(c)


def _tile_parent(p, n, offset=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if p.size == 0:
        return _binomial_a(n)
    P = p.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= P:
        # Use a window from the periodically extended parent; this keeps
        # valid nonnegative coefficients and preserves useful spectral shape.
        reps = (n + 3 * P) // P + 3
        long = np.tile(p, reps)
        off = int(offset) % max(1, P)
        if off + n > long.size:
            long = np.concatenate([long, np.tile(p, (off + n - long.size) // P + 2)])
        a = long[off : off + n]
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.pad(a, (0, n - a.size), mode="edge")
    else:
        reps = (n + 3 * P) // P + 3
        long = np.tile(p, reps)
        off = int(offset) % max(1, P)
        if off + n > long.size:
            long = np.concatenate([long, np.tile(p, (off + n - long.size) // P + 2)])
        a = long[off : off + n]
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.pad(a, (0, n - a.size), mode="edge")
    return _normalize(np.maximum(a, 0.0))


def _phase_best_parent(parent, n, fc):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if parent.size == 0:
        return _binomial_a(n)
    parent = _normalize(parent)
    best = None
    best_s = math.inf
    L = parent.size
    for base in (parent, parent[::-1]):
        base = _normalize(base)
        reps = (n + 3 * L) // max(1, L) + 3
        long = np.tile(base, reps)
        step = max(1, L // 300)
        for off in range(0, L, step):
            if off + n > long.size:
                long = np.concatenate([long, np.tile(base, (off + n - long.size) // L + 2)])
            a = _normalize(long[off : off + n])
            s = _fast_score(a, n, fc)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                best_s = s
                best = a.copy()
    if best is None:
        best = _binomial_a(n)
    return best


def _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, scale=1.0, endpoint=True):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = max(5, min(50, n // 8))
    for _ in range(K):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2)))
        amp = float(rng.lognormal(mean=-0.6, sigma=0.5))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * f * i) / max(1, n) + ph)
    if endpoint and n > 1:
        t = i / float(n - 1)
        z += float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.7)) * (1.0 - 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t))
    z += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    std = float(np.std(z))
    target = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5)) * float(scale)
    if std > 1e-12:
        z *= target / std
    z -= float(z.mean())
    return np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)


def _random_start(n, var, parent):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321 + max(0, int(n)) * 1000003 + max(0, int(var)) * 104729)
    v = int(var) % 8

    if v == 0:
        z = _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, endpoint=True)
        return _softmax_from_z(z)

    if v == 1:
        z = _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, endpoint=True, scale=1.2)
        a = _softmax_from_z(z)
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
            off = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, parent.size)))
            base = _tile_parent(parent, n, offset=off)
            mix = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.85))
            a = mix * base + (1.0 - mix) * a
            a = _normalize(a)
        return a

    if v == 2:
        b = _binomial_a(n)
        z = np.log(np.maximum(b, 1e-300)) + 0.4 * _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, endpoint=False, scale=0.6)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        return _softmax_from_z(z)

    if v == 3:
        w = max(3, n // 20)
        z = np.cumsum(rng.standard_normal(n))
        if w < n:
            z = np.convolve(z, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")
        z -= float(z.mean())
        std = float(np.std(z))
        if std > 1e-12:
            z *= float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2)) / std
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
        z += float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.5)) * (1.0 - 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t))
        return _softmax_from_z(np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0))

    if v == 4:
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
            off = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, parent.size)))
            base = _tile_parent(parent, n, offset=off)
        else:
            base = _binomial_a(n)
        z = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))
        z += 0.5 * _rand_fourier_z(n, rng, endpoint=True, scale=0.5)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        return _softmax_from_z(z)

    if v == 5:
        b = _binomial_a(n)
        z = np.log(np.maximum(b, 1e-300))
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(3):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            z += amp * np.sin((2.0 * math.pi * f * i) / max(1, n) + ph)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        return _softmax_from_z(z)

    if v == 6:
        # Mid-low frequency interference, endpoint-favoring envelope.
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(max(4, n // 60)):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 4)))
            amp = float(rng.lognormal(mean=-0.8, sigma=0.5))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            z += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * f * i) / max(1, n) + ph)
        if n > 1:
            t = i / float(n - 1)
            z += float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9)) * (1.0 - 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t))
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        std = float(np.std(z))
        if std > 1e-12:
            z *= float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0)) / std
        return _softmax_from_z(np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0))

    # Default: endpoint-favoring window.
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
    A = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
    z = A * (1.0 - 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t)) + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return _softmax_from_z(np.clip(z - z.mean(), -8.0, 8.0))


def _lbfgs_stage(a, tau_rel, stop_time, maxiter, plans):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    _, fc, fg, startg, stopeg = plans
    now = time.monotonic()
    if stop_time <= now + 0.4:
        return a

    logs = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    anchor = int(np.argmin(np.abs(logs - float(np.median(logs)))))
    mask = np.arange(n) != anchor
    v0 = (logs - logs[anchor])[mask].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    v0 = np.clip(v0, -50.0, 50.0)

    last_x = [v0.copy()]

    def callback(xk):
        try:
            last_x[0] = np.asarray(xk, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return time.monotonic() > stop_time

    def fun_jac(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        nloc = x.size
        zero_g = np.zeros(nloc, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.size != n - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return 1e100, zero_g

        zfull = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        zfull[anchor] = 0.0
        zfull[mask] = x

        a_cur = _softmax_from_z(zfull)
        if a_cur.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a_cur)):
            return 1e100, zero_g

        c = _fft_convolve(a_cur, n, fc)
        m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, zero_g

        tau = float(tau_rel) * m
        if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
            tau = max(1e-12, m * 1e-12)

        xexp = (c - m) / tau
        xexp = np.clip(xexp, -745.0, 0.0)
        ex = np.exp(xexp)
        ss = float(ex.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, zero_g
        p = ex / ss
        U = m + tau * math.log(ss)

        g = 2.0 * _fft_cross_grad(p, a_cur, n, fg, startg, stopeg)
        if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g[g < 0.0] = 0.0
        S = float(np.dot(a_cur, g))
        if not np.isfinite(S):
            S = 0.0
        h = a_cur * (g - S)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        gv = h[mask]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)):
            gv = np.where(np.isfinite(gv), gv, 0.0)

        score = float(2.0 * n * U)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return 1e100, zero_g
        return score, gv

    try:
        opts = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-5,
            "maxfun": int(max(80, 5 * maxiter)),
            "maxls": 5,
            "maxcor": 20,
        }
        res = sopt.minimize(fun_jac, v0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, callback=callback, options=opts)
        v_final = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if v_final is None:
            v_final = last_x[0]
        v_final = np.asarray(v_final, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v_final.size != n - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(v_final)):
            v_final = last_x[0]
    except Exception:
        v_final = last_x[0]

    zfull = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    zfull[anchor] = 0.0
    zfull[mask] = np.clip(v_final, -50.0, 50.0)
    a_new = _softmax_from_z(zfull)
    if a_new.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
        return a
    return a_new


def _local_search(a, stop_time, n, fc):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_s = _fast_score(a, n, fc)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        return best, best_s

    z = _z_from_a(a, mean_zero=True)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(555 + n * 97)
    step_sizes = (0.8, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005)
    kmax = max(16, min(64, n // 3))

    for _ in range(800):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.0:
            break
        c = _fft_convolve(a, n, fc)
        m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        if c.size <= kmax:
            idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(-c, kmax)[:kmax].astype(int)

        ct = c[idx]
        mt = float(ct.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mt) or mt <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-9, 0.01 * mt)
        ex = np.exp(np.clip((ct - mt) / tau, -745.0, 0.0))
        ss = float(ex.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones_like(ct, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, ct.size)
        else:
            p = ex / ss

        hg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k, w in zip(idx, p):
            w = float(w)
            if w <= 1e-11:
                continue
            k = int(k)
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if j1 < j0:
                continue
            j = np.arange(j0, j1 + 1, dtype=int)
            hg[j0 : j1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[k - j]

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hg)):
            break
        S = float(np.dot(a, hg))
        if not np.isfinite(S):
            S = 0.0
        h = a * (hg - S)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        mh = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mh) or mh <= 1e-10:
            break
        d = h / mh

        improved = False
        for st in step_sizes:
            zc = z - st * d
            ac = _softmax_from_z(zc)
            sc = _fast_score(ac, n, fc)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                z = zc
                a = ac.copy()
                best = ac.copy()
                best_s = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            z += (0.05 / math.sqrt(max(1, n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z -= float(z.mean())
            z = np.clip(z, -10.0, 10.0)

    return best, best_s


def _optimize_start(a0, n, stop_time, plans, consider, search_deadline):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    _, fc, _, _, _ = plans
    stop_time = min(float(stop_time), float(search_deadline) - 2.0)

    a_cur = a0.copy()
    a_best = a0.copy()
    best_fast = _fast_score(a0, n, fc)
    consider(a0, fast=best_fast, fc=fc)

    if n <= 384:
        tau_list = (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045, 0.022, 0.011, 0.0055, 0.0027)
        maxiter = 200
    elif n <= 1024:
        tau_list = (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045, 0.022, 0.011, 0.0055)
        maxiter = 130
    else:
        tau_list = (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045, 0.022)
        maxiter = 80

    for i, tr in enumerate(tau_list):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.2:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, stop_time - time.monotonic())
        stage_stop = min(stop_time - 0.3, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, rem / max(1, len(tau_list) - i)))
        if stage_stop <= time.monotonic() + 0.5:
            break
        a_new = _lbfgs_stage(a_cur, tr, stage_stop, maxiter, plans)
        if a_new is None:
            break
        a_new = _normalize(a_new)
        s_new = _fast_score(a_new, n, fc)
        consider(a_new, fast=s_new, fc=fc)

        if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_fast:
            best_fast = s_new
            a_best = a_new.copy()

        # Continue from the new iterate if it is not an obvious outlier;
        # otherwise revert to the best fast-known iterate in this start.
        if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < max(best_fast * 1.010, best_fast + 1e-9):
            a_cur = a_new
        else:
            a_cur = a_best

        sym = 0.5 * (a_cur + a_cur[::-1])
        consider(sym, fc=fc)

    if n <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.5:
        local_stop = min(stop_time - 0.5, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, (stop_time - time.monotonic()) / 3.0))
        a_loc, s_loc = _local_search(a_cur, local_stop, n, fc)
        consider(a_loc, fast=s_loc, fc=fc)
        if np.isfinite(s_loc) and s_loc < best_fast:
            best_fast = s_loc
            a_best = a_loc.copy()

    sym2 = 0.5 * (a_best + a_best[::-1])
    consider(sym2, fc=fc)
    return a_best, best_fast


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0

    if budget > 120.0:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 10.0)
        search_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 70.0)
    else:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - max(1.0, 0.15 * budget))
        search_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget * 0.45)
    search_deadline = min(search_deadline, hard_deadline - 8.0)

    parent = _get_parent()
    if parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
    parent = _normalize(parent)

    # Exact fallback.
    exact_seq = parent.copy()
    exact_score = _exact_score(parent)

    fallback = _binomial_a(64)
    fallback_score = _exact_score(fallback)
    if not np.isfinite(fallback_score):
        fallback = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        fallback_score = 2.0

    if not np.isfinite(exact_score):
        exact_seq = fallback.copy()
        exact_score = fallback_score

    base_plan = _fft_plans(parent.size)
    fast_seq = parent.copy()
    fast_score = _fast_score(parent, parent.size, base_plan[1])
    if not np.isfinite(fast_score):
        fast_score = exact_score
        fast_seq = exact_seq.copy()

    def consider(a, fast=None, fc=None):
        nonlocal fast_seq, fast_score, exact_seq, exact_score
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return
        if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return
        a = _normalize(a)
        n = a.size
        if fc is None:
            fc = _fft_plans(n)[1]
        if fast is None:
            fast = _fast_score(a, n, fc)
        if not np.isfinite(fast):
            return

        if fast < fast_score - 1e-14:
            fast_seq = a.copy()
            fast_score = float(fast)

        cands = [a]
        if n > 1:
            sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
            cands.append(sym)

        for c in cands:
            s = float(fast) if c is a else _fast_score(c, n, fc)
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            if s >= fast_score and s >= exact_score:
                continue
            # Exact validation only when there is plausible improvement and
            # enough reserve left. Final validation handles the eventual return.
            if (
                c.size <= 4096
                and time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 12.0
                and (s < exact_score - 1e-8 or s < 0.9995 * exact_score)
            ):
                es = _exact_score(c)
                if np.isfinite(es) and es < exact_score - 1e-12:
                    exact_seq = c.copy()
                    exact_score = float(es)
                    if s < fast_score - 1e-14:
                        fast_seq = c.copy()
                        fast_score = float(s)

    def try_shifts(a):
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return
        if a.size < 2 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return
        a = _normalize(a)
        n = a.size
        fc = _fft_plans(n)[1]
        base = a.copy()
        bs = _fast_score(base, n, fc)

        sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        ss = _fast_score(sym, n, fc)
        if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < bs:
            base = sym.copy()
            bs = ss

        step = max(1, n // 256)
        cnt = 0
        limit = max(0, int(search_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        for off in range(0, n, step):
            if cnt > 1000 or time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 4.0:
                break
            b = np.roll(base, off)
            s = _fast_score(b, n, fc)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < bs:
                bs = s
                base = b.copy()
            cnt += 1

        if base.size == n:
            consider(base, fast=bs, fc=fc)

    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 10.0:
        base_n = int(parent.size) if parent.size > 0 else 192
        if base_n > 4096:
            base_n = 192
        if base_n < 48:
            base_n = 128

        raw = []
        for f in (0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0, 8.0):
            raw.append(int(round(base_n * f)))
        for L in (128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096):
            raw.append(int(L))

        seen = set()
        temp = []
        for L in raw:
            L = int(L)
            if 64 <= L <= 4096 and L not in seen:
                seen.add(L)
                temp.append(L)
        if base_n not in temp:
            temp.insert(0, int(base_n))

        others = sorted(
            [L for L in temp if L != base_n],
            key=lambda L: (abs(math.log(max(1, L) / float(base_n))), int(L)),
        )
        lengths = [int(base_n)] + others[:12]

        weights = []
        for i, L in enumerate(lengths):
            if i == 0:
                w = 5.0
            else:
                r = abs(math.log(float(L) / float(base_n)))
                if r < 0.30:
                    w = 1.6
                elif r < 0.70:
                    w = 1.1
                elif r < 1.25:
                    w = 0.65
                elif r < 1.90:
                    w = 0.40
                else:
                    w = 0.25
            weights.append(w)

        remaining_weight = sum(weights)
        total_weight = max(1e-12, remaining_weight)
        end_time = search_deadline - 5.0

        for ni, nL in enumerate(lengths):
            if time.monotonic() > end_time:
                break
            rem_time = max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic())
            if rem_time < 8.0:
                break
            wnorm = weights[ni]
            if remaining_weight <= 0:
                wnorm = 1.0
                tw = wnorm
            else:
                tw = remaining_weight
                remaining_weight -= weights[ni]
            alloc = rem_time * (wnorm / max(1e-12, tw))
            length_stop = min(end_time, time.monotonic() + max(6.0, alloc))
            if length_stop <= time.monotonic() + 2.5:
                continue

            plans = _fft_plans(int(nL))
            nL = int(nL)
            fc = plans[1]

            base_starts = []
            if parent.size > 0 and length_stop - time.monotonic() > 12.0:
                try:
                    base_starts.append(_phase_best_parent(parent, nL, fc))
                except Exception:
                    pass
                try:
                    base_starts.append(_phase_best_parent(parent[::-1], nL, fc))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            try:
                base_starts.append(_binomial_a(nL))
                base_starts.append(_binomial_a(nL)[::-1])
            except Exception:
                base_starts.append(_binomial_a(nL))

            if nL <= 256:
                planned = 34
                max_starts = planned * 3
            elif nL <= 768:
                planned = 18
                max_starts = planned * 3
            elif nL <= 2048:
                planned = 9
                max_starts = planned * 3
            else:
                planned = 5
                max_starts = planned * 3

            idx = 0
            while time.monotonic() < length_stop - 2.0 and idx < max_starts:
                rem_len = max(0.0, length_stop - time.monotonic())
                if rem_len < 3.0:
                    break
                if idx < len(base_starts):
                    a0 = base_starts[idx]
                else:
                    a0 = _random_start(nL, idx - len(base_starts), parent)
                idx += 1

                rem_index = max(1, planned - idx)
                start_stop = min(length_stop - 0.3, time.monotonic() + max(2.5, rem_len / rem_index))
                try:
                    _optimize_start(a0, nL, start_stop, plans, consider, search_deadline)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if time.monotonic() > length_stop - 1.0:
                break

    # Shift/symmetrization polish on the best candidates.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 35.0:
        try:
            try_shifts(fast_seq)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            try_shifts(exact_seq)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final exact validation. Prefer the best fast candidate if it validates
    # better than the stored exact champion; otherwise return the exact champion.
    final_ret = exact_seq
    try:
        if np.isfinite(fast_score) and np.isfinite(exact_score) and fast_score < exact_score - 1e-9:
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 3.0 and fast_seq is not None and fast_seq.size <= 8192:
                fes = _exact_score(fast_seq)
                if np.isfinite(fes) and fes < exact_score:
                    exact_score = float(fes)
                    final_ret = fast_seq
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        final_ret = np.asarray(final_ret, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        final_ret = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    if final_ret.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(final_ret).all():
        final_ret = fallback.copy()

    # One last exact check of the exact object we actually return if time permits.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 1.0:
            final_es = _exact_score(final_ret)
            if np.isfinite(final_es):
                final_ret = final_ret.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    final_ret = np.clip(np.asarray(final_ret, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_ret.size == 0:
        final_ret = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in final_ret]
```
